Ukungenisa izinto ezweni kanye nokuthumela
Isicelo sokungenisa imikhiqizo yederi (yobisi) ngaphakathi ezweni noma ukuhambisa ngokudlulisa eNingizimu Afrika
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Lezi zimvume zokungenisa impahla (import permit) zibekelwe umkhawulo wesikhathi esithize, kanye nokungeniswa kanye.
Yenza isicelo semvume okungenani amaviki ayisithupha kodwa hhayi angaphezulu kwayisishagalombili ngaphambi kwenyathelo olihlosile.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo
Uma imikhiqizo ihamba ngokudluliswa eRiphabhliki yeNingizimu Afrika, kumele kube nekhophi yemvume kadokotela wezilwane ebuya kulelo lizwe lapho lo mkhiqizo osiwa khona, le khophi yemvume kumele inikezwe ihambisana nesicelo semvume yokudlusa umkhiqizo, isicelo sinikezwe udokotela wezilwane weNingizimu Afrika.
Imali yediphozithi kumele ifakelwa kwi-akhawunti yebhangi loMnyango wezoLimo:
Igatsha: 010845
Sicela niqaphele: Umngenisi womkhiqizo, unomsebenzi emahlombe akhe wokukhokhela amashaji ebhangi, kanye namashaji eBhangi langaPhandle uMnyango ngeke wakhipha imvume uma inani eligcwele elingamarandi ayikhulu (ZAR115) lingakakhokhelwa.
Imali ingakhokhelwa nasemahhovisi loMnyango wezolimo, uhla lawo olufakelwe kamuva esigabeni esithi: Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana.
Uma wenza isicelo egameni lomunye umuntu, sicela uqinisekise ukuthi ukhokha egameni lalowo muntu noma leyo nkampani.
Asikho isicelo esiyobonelelwa ngaphandle kobufakazi bokukhokha. Ubufakazi bokukhokha kumele buthunyelwe ngefekisi kule nombolo ethi: 012 329 8292, iqondiswe ku: Ina Labuschagne.
Chaza kwifomu lesicelo ukuthi ngabe uzozilandela imvume noma kumele uposelwe yona. Qinisekisa nabehhovisi lemvume ngocingo ukuthi imvume isilungile ngaphambi kokuba uyoyilanda.
eThekwini: Ucingo: 031 337 4346/7
Ucingo: 011 613 1636
Izidingo zokungenisa imikhiqizo yenyama zitholakala ehhovisi lemvume noma kwi-website yoMnyango wezoLimo ekhelini loMnyango wezolimo.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kuthatha izinsuku ezimbili ukuya kwezintathu zokusebenza ukubhekana nesicelo semvume uma abempilo yezilwane besivuma.

Indawo yokuhlala
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Abarejista (ababhalisa) amatayitela bavumela noma yiliphi ilunga lomphakathi ukuthola ulwazi maqondana nokulandelayo:
umnikazi wempahla (weprophathi) obhalisile (orejistile)
izimo ezichaphazela leyo mpahla (prophathi)
imiyalelo yenkantolo yokuvimbela ukwenziwa kokuthize (interdicts) maqondana nempahla (neprophathi)
inani lentengiso yempahla (yeprophathi)
zimo ngokusungulwa kwendawo yokuhlala yabantu ulwazi oluqondene nempahla noma itayitela.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Ngaphambi kokuba kutholakale ulwazi kubabhalisi bamatayitela, kumele ube nalokhu okulandelayo:
o amagama aphelele/ noma inombolo kamazisi yomnikazi wempahla, noma okungenani usuku lwakhe lokuzalwa. (Kwisimo lapho ubhekana nalokho okuthathwa njengomuntu ngokomthetho - juristic person, igama kanye nenombolo yerejistreshini - yobhaliso, uma kukhona, kuyadingeka)
o inombolo yesitende ye-erf kanye nenombolo ye-township, noma igama lepulazi kanye nenombolo, kodwa hhayi ikheli lomgwaqo. (Lapho kuyisikimu setayitela eyingxenye yempahla - sectional title scheme, isigaba, kanye negama lesikimu kuyadingeka.)
Ukuthola ikhophi yetayitela noma idokhumende evela kubabhalisi bamatayitela, kumele wenze okulandelayo:
o hamba uye kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lematayitela (ababhalisi bamatayitela kungenzeka banganikezi ulwazi olucelwa ngencwadi noma ngokushaya ucingo)
o hamba uye kwideski lolwazi, lapho khona isikhulu sizosizana nawe ukugcwalisa ifomu elibekiwe kanye nokuchaza inqubo o cela u-data typist ukuthi akuseshele ulwazi ngempahla (ngeprophathi), noma o khokha inani lemali efanele kwihhovisi elamukela imali ebese uthatha isiliphu usibuyisela kusikhulu kwideski yolwazi (information desk). Inombolo yerisidi izofakelwa kwikhophi yakho yetayitela.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ukusesha ulwazi kungathatha imizuzu engu-30 ukuya kwengu-60. Kwamanye amahhovisi amakhulu, ikhophi yetayitela iyaposwa, noma kumele izolandwa ngemuva kwesikhathi esithize.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Ikhophi yesivumelwano esenziwa phambi komendo (antenuptual contract) noma itayitela ngenhloso yolwazi:  Ngu-R36
Ikhophi yezimo ngokusungulwa kwendawo yokuhlala yabantu: Ngu-R4 ikhasi
Ukusesha itayitela: Ngu-R6

Izincazelo
I-ISS isizana nabantu ngamunye kanye nemindeni abanezinkinga umuntu ngamunye siqu noma zemiphakathi, abadinga usizo lokusebenzisana phakathi kwamazwe. Lulu sizo lutholakala kunoma yimuphi umuntu oseNingizimu Afrika noma phesheya.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Izicelo zabantu abahlala eNingizimu Afrika:
Yenza isicelo ehhovisi eliseduzane nawe likasonhlalonhle
Izicelo zabantu abahlala ngaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika:
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Lapho kwamukelwe isicelo sokuthunyelwa esibuya ezweni langaphandle, abe-ISS SA badlulisela udaba kummeli wesifundazwe wendawo ozokwenza amalungiselelo nosonhlalonhle ukuphenyisisa ngalo kanye nokunikeza umbiko kwabomnyango wesifundazwe. Uma umbiko usuwamukelwe, uthunyelwa kwabe-ISS SA, okuyibona abawuhlaziyayo kanye nokudlulisela umbiko kwabathintekayo (stakeholders), umbiko ozohambisana nokuphawula kwabo, imibuzo noma ukuphengulula (queries).
Phezulu
Izindleko
ENingizimu Afrika, inkonzo inikezelwa mahhala. Kwamanye amazwe, ungakhokhiswa imali yezinye izinkonzo. Lezi zimali ezikhokhelwayo ziyahluka kuzwe nezwe.
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Awekho amafomu okumele agcwaliswe.

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi

Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Umhlaba wombuso (umhlaba ongaphansi kolawulo loMnyango wezeMihlaba noma weMisebenzi kaHulumeni emiphakathini noma uhulumeni wesifundazwe - weprovinsi noma kamasipala) kanye nomhlaba onabanikazi bangasese ungangenelwa (invaded) ngokungemthetho.
Uhulumeni unomsebenzi ngokomthethosisekelo ukuqinisekisa ukuthi -  lapho kunesidingo khona, kanti futhi lapho kunemithombo yosizo eyenele - zonke izakhamizi zingafinyelela kumalungelo azo okuthola umhlaba kanye/noma izindlu. Kwizimo ezithile ezifana nalapho iningi labantu lingena emhlabeni ngokungemthetho, uhulumeni unomsebenzi wokusizana nomnikazi womhlaba ukuvikela umhlaba wakhe.
Abantu ziqu (individuals) (ababandakanya labo abahlala ngokungemthetho), abanganayo imihlaba yolimo bangaxhumana noMnyango wezeMihlaba ukuthola usizo ukuthola umhlaba ofanele wolimo. Abantu ziqu (individuals) abadinga izindlu bangaxhumana nomasipala wendawo noma, uma kunesidingo, umnyango wezezindlu kahulumeni wesifundazwe (weprovinsi) noma uMnyango kazwelonke wezeZindlu, ukuthola usizo.
uzothintana nenkantolo ukuthola umyalelo wokukhipha abahlezi ngokungemthetho kumhlaba wombuso ongaphansi kolawulo lwawo inqaba nje uma amalungelo abahlezi ngokungemthetho engathikamezeki usize abahlezi emhlabeni wombuso ngokungemthetho abadinga nabangenawo umhlaba wolimo ukuthola umhlaba wolimo ofanele usize ezinye iziphathimandla zikaulumeni ukuthola omunye umhlaba ofanele onganikezwa abahlezi ngokungemthetho, abadinga izindlu lapho kudingeke khona, nalapho kunezimo ezingavamile, ukusiza abantu bangasese abangabanikazi bomhlaba wolimo umhlaba wabo ongenelwe ngokungemthetho ukususa labo abahlezi kuwo ngokungemthetho abadinga umhlaba wolimo ofanele ukuya komunye umhlaba ofanele.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Lapho khona umhlaba womuntu wangasese ungenelwe ngokungemthetho, kanti kungekho izimo ezingavamile, umnikazi wenkantolo angathintana naboMnyango wezeMihlaba ukuthola usizo, uma isidingo esibangelwe ukungenelwa komhlaba ngokungemthetho kuyidisingo sokuthola umhlaba wolimo.
Lapho umhlaba wangasese ungenelwe ngokungemthetho kanti kukhona izimo ezingavamile, umnikazi womhlaba angaxhumana nomasipala wendawo, noma uma kufanele, umnyango wesifundazwe (weprovinsi) noma uMnyango kazwelonke wezeZindlu, ukuthola usizo uma isidingo esibangele lokho kungenelwa ngokungemthetho kuyisidingo sezindlu.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ekuqaleni, kungathatha abophiko lwe-Directorate: Ukusekelwa kwabantu ngoMhlaba izinsuku ezingu-14, ukufuna kanye nokubeka omunye umhlaba ofanele. Kodwa-ke, ngenxa yesidingo sokubonisana nabathintekayo kanye nokwamukelwa ngungqongqoshe, inqubo ingathatha cishe izinyanga ezimbili ukuya kwezintathu ukuthi iphethwe.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Izindleko ezibandakanyekayo ekutholeni omunye umhlaba ofanele, kuya ngesimo nesimo. Izinto ezifana nobungakanani bomphakathi ofunelwa umhlaba, kanye nezindleko zomhlaba kuleyo ndawo nakho kuyabonelelwa.
Phezulu

Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ilayisensi yokufundela ukushayela yilayisensi yokwesikhashana efakazela ukuthi unolwazi oluncane lokushayela imoto. Amalayisensi okufundela ukushayela ahlukaniswe izigaba ezintathu. Zonke izigaba zibekelwe iminyaka ngokwehlukahlukana.
Isigaba sokuqala: Isithuthuthu noma ngayisiphi
Isigaba sesibili: Imoto encane
Isigaba sesithathu: enye nenye imoto - iminyaka engu 18
Abafake izicelo abangeke bavivinywe ngosuku olubekiwe, kanti-ke nesikhathi sokulindela ukuvivinywa siyashiyana ngabomasipala.
Ilayisensi yokufundela ukushayela iyaphela emuva kwezinyanga ezingu 18 kanti izinsuku noma izinyanga ezengezwa. 
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma ufuna ukufaka isicelo gcwalisa ifomu (LL1) esiteshini sokuvivinya esiseduze nawe.
Woza nomazisi walkho omukelekile kanye namakhophi njengokusho komthetho wezinqola ezihamba emgwaqweni kazwelonke ka 1996, umazisi, noma umazisi wesikhashana noma ipasipoti.
Izithombe ezingezona zombala zencwadi yomazisi ezimbili.
kuzomele ukuthi uhlolwe amehlo esiteshini sokuvivinya.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ilayisense yokufunda itholalakala ngalo usuku obhale ilayisense yakho.

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Ezokuthutha nokuhamba
Isicelo sencwadi yesigunyaziso maqondana nemoto
Ukufaka isicelo sencwadi yokugunyaza ephathelene nemoto
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Umele ukuthi uthole incwadi yokugunyaza uma ufuna ukwenza loku okulandelayo ukuthenga imoto entsha noma ese isebenzile kwamanye amazwe, ukuthenga imoto yezimpahla noma inqola edonswa ngenye nokwakha noma ukuguqula kancane imoto.
Umele ukuthi ube nencwadi yokugunyaza noma imoto yakhiwe kuleli noma yakhiwe kwelinye izwe. Izinhlobo zezimoto okudingeka incwadi yokugunyaza kuzona:
izimoto, amabhasi kanye nezithuthuthu izimoto ezithwala izimpahla (amaloli namaveni)
izinqola ezidoswa ngemuva kwezimoto izimoto eziyisipesheli nje ngoJibha ohambayo, ogandaganda nezimoto zokuvuna.
Zonke izimoto, noma zakhiwe lapha eMzansi noma kwamanye amazwe kumele zilandele amazinga abekwe ngumthetho wezinqola ezihamba emgwaqweni kazwelonke ka 1996. abathenga izimoto ngaphandle kwaleli kumele baveze ubufakazi bokuthi zilandele amazinga abekwe ngumthetho wezinqola ezihamba emgwaqweni kazwelonke ka 1996 babuse e SABS okuyondawo ebhekene namazinga okwenziwa kwemisebenzi eMzansi.
Isixwayiso: ifomu ligcwaliswa ngumuntu imoto ibhaliswe ngaye.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa amafomu uthumele nalamaphepha alandelayo:
o ikhophi yomazisi o incwadi yamandla okumela uma ngabe yinkampani noma yinhlangano enkulu o iafidaviti evela kumaphoyisa aseMzansi uma kuyimoto eyakhiwe. Loku kefanele kufake phakathi nenombholo yenjini, neyechesi, nemininingwane yonke yomsebenzi owenziwe nokuthi izinsimbi zitholakale kuphi.
o nesitifiketi semvume yamaphoyisa o nesitifiketi semvume yokuthi imoto ihambe emgwaqweni o nesitifiketi sesisindo semoto o izithombe ezimbili zemoto.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Lencwadi yokugunyaza izobe isilungile ezinsukwini ezine kuya kweziyisithupha.

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Isicelo sokuvula i-akhawunti yeBhangi yeMzansi
Isimo
I-Akhawunti yeMzansi, yinkonzo ebhekwe ngabe-SAPO (South African Post Office) eqondene nabantu ziqu. Yindlela elula yokwamukela imali kanye nokukhipha imali noma ukukhokha. Ungaba nesiqiniseko sokuthi imali yakho iphephile ngoba akukho zindleko zenyanga, okugcina ku-akhawunti yakho, kuzothola inzalo. Akukho zimali ezikhokhwa njalo, ukhokhela nje lokho okusebenzisile. Awudingi isiliphi somholo ukuvula i-akhawunti yoMzansi. Noma ngubani angavula i-akhawunti, ngoba akukho mibandela efana nokuba nemali ethize yomholo okumele uyihole edingekayo ukuvula le-akhawunti. Ungasebenzisa ibhangi kumagatsha ePosi angu-1300 noma kumishini yama-ATM (Automated Teller Machines) engu-8000.
Konke ukufakela izimali ePosini kwenziwa mahhala.
Qaphela: Abasebancane ngeminyaka abafisa ukuvula le-akhawunti kumele bahambe nomuntu omdala ongumnakekeli ngokomthetho noma umzali/abazali.
Ukuze uthole olunye ulwazi, ngena kwi iposini
Inqubo okumele ilandelwe
Ukuvula i-akhawunti, okudingayo nje kuphela, ngokulandelayo:
Hamba uye ePosini eliseduzane eNingizimu Afrika ucele u-teller (umsizi waseposini) ukukunikeza ifomu lesicelo.
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo.
Khombisa uMazisi wakho (i-ID yakho).
Khombisa ubufakazi bendawo ohlala kuyo. Njengengxenye yomthetho we-Financial Intelligence Centre Act (FICA), bonke abenzi bezicelo kumele banikeze ubufakazi bendawo abahlala kuyo. Lokhu kungenziwa ngokuthi ukhombise isitatimende sokukhokhela okusebenzisayo (kungaba yi-akhawunti kamasipala). Uma uqashile (urentile), ikhophi yekontraki yokuqasha (yokurenta) iyadingeka. Uma uhlala nabazali, isitatimende esifungelwe (i-afidavithi) efakazela lokhu iyadingeka.
Abasebancane ngeminyaka abafisa ukuvula le-akhawunti kumele bahambe nomuntu omdala ongumnakekeli ngokomthetho noma umzali/abazali.

Izincazelo
Inhloso yale nkonzo (isevisi) ukwazisa u-Registrar of Companies (umbhalisi wezinkampani) ngoshintsho kwibhizinisi lenu elibhalisiwe. Lolu shintsho (lezi zinguquko) zingasukela ezintweni ezifana nokungezela amalungu amasha, noma ukuhoxa kwamalunga, kuye ku-accounting iHhovisir omusha, ikheli lokuposa, njalonjalo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gudluzela ifomu lesicelo le-CK2 nigcwalise izigaba ezisebenza kulesi simo senu.
Gcwalisani i-Part A uma kukhona ushintsho egameni le-CC noma inhlobo yebhizinisi.
Nikezani ngekhophi eqinisekisiwe yesitifikedi sobhaliso (serejistreshini) ye-CC.
Khokhelani imali edingekayo ebekiwe.

Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Abaqashi kumele bagcwalise ingxenye yokuqala noma isigaba A sefomu u W.Cl.2.
Abaqashi kumele badabule isigaba B sefomu u W.Cl.2 balinikeze udokodela noma isibhedlela ngokushesha emva kokwenzeka kwengozi.
Abaqashi kumele bathumele isigaba A sefomu u W.Cl.2 kubesikhwama sesincephezelo ngokushesha emva kokwenzeka kwengozi.
Abaqashi abangalindi isigaba B sefomu ukuthi sigcwaliswe ngudokodela anduba bafake isicelo sokuncephezelwa. Bazathi bangalithola isigaba B sefomu elivela kudokodela, bese balidlulisela phambili.
Abaqashi kumele bakhokhele umsebenzi imali yesincephezelo yezinyanga ezintathu zokuqala emva kokwenzeka kwengozi. Abesikhwama sesincephezelo bazombuyisela imali yakhe umqashi ngaloku.
Uma umsebenzi engeke akwazi ukubuyela emsebenzini masishane, abaqashi kumele bathole amaripoti esimo sesigulane (W.Cl.5)  kudokodela bawuthumele kubesikhwama sesincephezelo njalo nganyanga.
Uma umsebenzi ebuyela emsebenzini, abaqashi kumele bathumele iripoti yokubuyela emsebenzini (W.Cl.6), kanye neripoti yokugcina (W.Cl.5) kubesikhwama sesincephezelo.

Isicelo semvume yokuhlala unomphela - kumnikazi webhizinisi
Izincazelo

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi

Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) isebenza kumuntu wangaphandle onenhloso yokuzosungula ibhizinisi eNingizimu Afrika; noma osevele enemvume yebhizinisi ukuqhuba ibhizinisi ezweni; noma onenhloso yokuzotshala izimali kwibhizinisi elikhona ezweni.
Izicelo zingenziwa kunoma yiliph ihhovisi laboMnyango wezaseKhaya kwiprovinsi (kusifundazwe) onenhloso yokuqhuba ibhizinisi kuso. Yenza isicelo kwinxusa (embassy) noma i-mission uma ungaphandle kwezwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo, i-BI-947 no-BI-29.
U-chartered accountant osebenza egameni lakho kumele afakele isiqiniseko sokuthi ngempela ifakelwe imali elingana umkhawulo obekiwe ezincwadini (book value) webhizinisi. Umkhawulo wemali okunqunywe ukuthi ifakelwe yinani lika-R2,5 miliyoni kanti futhi leli nani lingabekelwa eceleni kuphela noma liphungulwe uma lokhu kuzobonelela izidingo zesizwe (national interest), noma uma kucela aboMnyango wezoHwebo neZimboni ukwenze njalo.
Uma sekutholakele imvume yokuhlala unomphela, kumele uvuselele isiqiniseko se-chartered accountant eminyakeni emibili ngemuva kosuku lokukhishwa kwemvume yokuhlala unomphela, kanti futhi kweminye iminyaka emithathu ngemuva kwalokho. Ukuhluleka ukulandela lo mbandela kuyoba nomphumela wokuthi imvume yakho yokuhlala unomphela iphele.

Le nkonzo isebenza kumuntu owemukelwe njengombaleki odinga ukukhosela eNingizimu Afrika. Izicelo zingenziwa ehhovisi eliseduzane labezindaba zaseKhaya kwisifundazwe (kwiprovinsi) ohlala kuyo Kumele ube usebe nesimo sokuba ngumkhoseli iminyake emihlanu ukuze wenze isicelo.

Izincazelo
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) itholakala kubantu bangaphandle abangabalingani nobudlelwane nezakhamizi zaseNingizimu Afrika noma abanelungelo lokuhlala unomphela.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo, i-BI-947 no-BI-29.
Ubudlelwana banomphela bophathina phakathi kwabantu abaganene noma abahlalisene ngobulili obufanayo (homosexual) kanye nabobulili obehlukene (heterosexual) bamukelekile, kodwa kunokuba nesitifikedi somshado labo phathina kudingeka ukuthi bachaze kabanzi ngokuhlalisana kwabo, kanye nokusekelana kwabo kanye nokusekelana ngezimali ngokuletha incwadi efungelwe kwabomthetho (i-afidavithi).
Ukuze aboMnyango wezaseKhaya bakwazi ukunikeza imvume yokuhlala unomphela kumlingani wangaphandle kusakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika noma lowo onelungelo lokuhlala unomphela, kumele baneliseke ngokuthi kukhona ubudlelwane bangempela.
Imvume yokuhlala unomphela ikhishwa ngaphansi kwesimo sokuthi iyophela uma kutholakala ukuthi eminyakeni emibili ubudlelwane abusekho, ngaphandle kwalapho kuwukuthi omunye umlingani ushonile.
Ngumlingani oyedwa kuphela wangaphandle ongenza isicelo sokuganana kulesi simo ngokuthi uganene nomuntu oyisakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika noma umuntu onelungelo lokuhlala unomphela
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ngeyamahhala ayikhokhelwa.

Izincazelo
Le nkonzo isebenza kumuntu wangaphandle ofisa ukwenza isicelo sokuba ngumhlali wanomphela ngenxa yokuthi enikezwe umsebenzi wanomphela eNingizimu Afrika. Isicelo kumele siwele ngaphansi komkhawulo wonyaka obekwayo ngezikhathi nezikhathi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo, i-BI-947 no-BI-29E.
Lowo ofuna ukukuqasha kumele anikeze ubufakazi bokulandelayo kwaboMnyango wezaseKhaya:
o ukuthi isikhundla somsebenzi onikezwa wona sikhona o ukuthi isikhundla kanye nencazelo yomsebenzi kwafakelwa ezazisweni zokukhangisa ngendlela ebekiwe.
AboMnyango wezemiSebenzi (Department of Labour) kumele baqinisekise ukuthi izimo nemibandela yomsebenzi azikho ngaphansi kwalezo ezinikezwa, nezikhona kulowo mkhakha womsebenzi abantu abayizakhamizi zaseNingizimu Afrika, nabanelungelo lokuhlala unomphela. Ngokwenza lokho, abe-DoL kumele babonelele izivumelwano zezingxoxo zokubonisana ndawonye phakathi kwabasebenzi nabaqashi (collective bargaining agreements) kanye namanye amazinga asebenza kwimikhakha nemikhakha.

Izincazelo
Iminyaka ejutshiwe yezigaba zezimoto iyalandela:
imoto ethwala izimpahla - iminyaka engu 18
imoto ethwala abantu - iminyaka engu 21
imoto ethwala izimpahla eziyingozi - iminyaka engu 25.
esasebenza yokushayela leyonhlobo yemoto upotsholwe ngudokodela wathi uphilile unikezwe igunya yinhlangano yokufundisa evumelekile (loku kwenzeka ezinhlobeni ezitile zezimoto)
ungakaze waboshelwa ukushayela uphuzile, noma ushayela ubudlabha noma waboshelwa icala eliphathelene nokulwa ilayisensi yakho ingakaze imiswe.
Kuyizinhlobo ezithile zamacala ezingenza ukuthi ungatholi iphomede yokushayela kobungcweti. Amacala osekuphele iminyaka emihlanu enzeka awazubhekwa.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu PD1 ulithumele esenta yokuvivinya abashayeli
Uzohlolwa amehlo esenta yokuvivinya abashayeli.
o incwadi yomazisi, ilayisensi yekhadi noma ipasipoti yaseMzansi esebenzayo.
o izithombe ezingezona zombala zencwadi yomazisi ezimbili o ilayisensi esebenzayo yokushayela o isitifiketi sokufunda uma kudingeka o isitifiketi sakwadokodela esingadluli ezinyangeni ezimbili senziwa o namanye amaphomede okushayela onawo.
Khokha imali enqunyiwe.
Uzokwaziswa uma ikhadi lephomede yokushayela kobungcweti selilungile ukuthi ulilande. Umele ukuthi ukwazi ukuzazisa uma ulanda ikhadi lakho. Amakhadi angalandwa kuze kuphele izinsuku ezingu 120 azobhuqwa.

Ubudlelwane nezobulili
Isicelo sokushintshwa noma ukugudlulela eceleni um
Izincazelo
Yini udlame lwasekhaya?
ukulimaza impahla ukulandelela ukungena endaweni yomuntu ngaphandle kwemvume yakhe noma yikuphi okunye ukuhlukunyezwa, noma ukulawulwa indlela oziphatha ngayo
Lapho lokho kuziphatha kwalowo muntu okukulimazayo noma okungabangela ukuthi ulimale empilweni yakho, ngokuphepha kwakho noma ngokungaphili kahle.
Uma lezi zinhlobo zokuhlukumezeka zenzeka kuwe noma komunye umuntu omaziyo, ungenza isicelo somyalelo wokuvikelwa. Umyalelo wokuvikelwa kudlame lwasekhaya, yidokhumende ekhishwa yinkantolo evimbela umhlukumezi ukwenza okulandelayo:
ukwenza isenzo sodlame lwasekhaya ukuthola usizo lomunye umuntu ukwenza isenzo esinjalo ukungena endaweni yokuhlala ehlanganyelwe ngumenzi wesicelo kanye nalowo okwenziwa ngaye isikhalazo ukungena engxenyeni ethile yaleyo ndawo yokuhlala ehlanganyelwe;
ukungena endaweni yokuhlala ehlala umenzi wesikhalazo;
ukungena endaweni lapho okusebenza khona umenzi wesicelo (ummangali);
ukuvimbela umenzi wesikhalazo (ummangali) ohlala noma obehlala endaweni ehlanganyelwe ukuthi angene endaweni yokuhlala ehlanganyelwe noma engxenyeni yendawo ehlanganyelwe yokuhlala noma engxenyeni ethile ebekwe njengengxenye yendawo yokuhlala; noma ukwenza noma yisiphi isenzo esifakwe kumyelelo wenkantolo wokuvikela (protection order).
Ngubani ongenza isicelo somyalelo wenkantolo wokuvikelwa?
Noma ngubani ohlukunyeziwe
Osemncane ngeminyaka angenza isicelo ngaphandle kosizo
Noma ngubani umuntu onakekela noma okhathazeka ngempilo yomenzi wesicelo (ummangali). Abantu abalandelayo bangenza isicelo ngokuvunyelwa ngumkhalazi (ummangali) ngokubhala (written consent), ngaphandle kokuba umkhalazi esemncane ngeminyaka, noma engumuntu ongaphilile kahle ngokwengqondo (mentally retarded person), osahanjelwe yingqondo noma oqulekile (unconscious), noma uma inkantolo ineliseka, wukuthi lowo muntu akakwazi ukunikezela ngokuvuma kwakhe.
Umsebenzi wezempilo
IPhoyisa
o uThisha (umfundisi)
Ukuthola olunye ulwazi, ngena kwi-website yaboMnyango wezoBulungisa nokuThuthukiswa koMthethosisekelo.
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza isicelo somyalelo wokuvikelwa eNkantolo kaMantshi eseduzane nalapho uhlala khona noma usebenza khona noma kunini, ngesikhathi samahora okusebenza noma ngaphandle kwesikhathi samahora okusebenza, ngisho nangamaholidi noma ngezimpelasondo
Isicelo kumele senziwe ngesitatimende esifungelwe kwabomthetho (i-afidavithi), echaza okulandelayo:
Uhlobo lomyalelo okwenziwa isicelo sawo
Igama lesiteshi samaphoyisa lapho umkhalazi (ummangali) angabika khona ukwephulwa komyalelo wokuvikelwa okwenziwa isicelo sawo.
Lapho khona isicelo esenziwa egameni lomunye umkhalazi, senziwa ngomunye umuntu, isitatimende esifungelwe kwabomthetho (i-afidavithi), kumele ichaze okulandelayo:
Indlela lowo muntu owenzela omunye isicelo akhathazeka ngayo ngempilo yalowo mkhalazi enza isicelo egameni lakhe
Umsebenzi owenziwa yilowo muntu (occupation), kanye nesikhundla noma isimo (capacity) enza isicelo ngaphansi kwaso
Ukuvuma kwalowo muntu okubhaliwe (written consent) okhalazayo, ngaphandle kokuba umkhalazi:
ekhubazekile ngokwengqondo;
esahanjelwe zingqondo noma equlekile (unconscious)
umuntu inkantolo eneliseka ngaye ngokuthi lowo muntu akakwazi ukwenza ukuvuma ngokwakhe noma ukunikeza imvume edingekayo.
Qinisekisa (certify) ifomu nomabhalane wenkantolo ebese uyifakela.
Lapho ifomu selemukelwe, umabhalane uzothumela isicelo sakho kumantshi ozobeka usuku lokuthi ubuyele enkantolo, ukuze kubonelelwe isicelo sakho.
Umantshi uzolungisa nenothisi ezokwazisa umhlukumezi ngesivikelo senkantolo, kanye nokumazisa ukuthi eze nini enkantolo.
Ngemuba kokuvela ngaphambi kwenkantolo, umantshi anganikeza umyalelo wokuvikelwa.
Imithetho nemigomo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi kanye nesikhathi sokwenziwa komsebenzi
Ezikhathini eziphuthumayo, inkonzo itholakala amahora angu-24
Umyalelo wokuvikela ungatholakala ngalo lolo suku, kodwa lokhu kuvamise ukwenzeka ngokubonelela nokuncika kwisixaka-xaka sodaba lwakho.
Umyalelo usebenza kuze kufike lapho umuntu ohlukunyeziwe ewukhansela khona.
Uma umhlukumezi efakela i-aphili, umyalelo uyaqhubeka nokusebenza kuze kufike lapho ukhanselwa khona yiNkantolo yama-Aphili.

Isicelo sokwamukelwa kokuhlanganiswa ngokwenkambiso yosiko noma umendo
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Umendo wenkambiso yosiko (custormay marriage) ukuze wamukelwe njengomshado osemthethweni, lo mendo wenkambiso yosiko kumele kube wenziwa kungakashayi umhlaka-15 Novemba 2000. Uma ngabe lo mendo wenziwa ukusukela ngomhlaka 15 Novemba ukuza nganeno, lowo mendo kumele ulandele izidingo ezilandelayo:
Umendo kumele wamukeleke noma ungenelwe ngokulandela umthetho wezamasiko (customary law).
Abazali boyedwa wabakanikazi osemncane ngeminyaka kumele bavumele umendo. Uma ungenabo abazali, umuntu okunakekelayo ngokomthetho kumele avume. Uma abazali noma umnakekeli ngokomthetho bengavumi, kungaxhunyanwa nokhomishana wezenhlalakahle yezingane ukuze avume. Uma imvume ingekho, okungaba ngeyabazali, umnakekeli ngokomthetho (legal guardian), noma ukhomishana wezingane, yijaji laseNkantolo ePhezulu elingabhekana nesicelo semvume.
Ngisho noma kungekho sihibe ngenani lemendo yenkambiso yesintu umyeni angangena kuyo, kodwa ngeke kwavunyelwa ukuthi kungenelwe omunye umendo wenkambiso yosiko ngaphandle kokuba kunomyalelo wenkantolo (court order) elawula ikusasa lenqubo yezempahla lomendo wakhe.
Qinisekisa isimo sakho kwezomendo ngokubheka online.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo le-BI-32 kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lezaseKhaya eNingizimu Afrika. Yenza isicelo kwinxusa (embassy) noma i-mission uma ungaphandle kwezwe.
Isitifikedi esiphelele (unabridged) somendo (somshado) noma ukwenziwa futhi kwerejista efanele noma ukwenziwa futhi kwanoma yimaphi amadokhumende ahambisana nerejista iyakhishwa kanye nesiqiniso solwazi ukusukela kwirejista yomshado.
Isitifikedi somshado (somendo) esifingqiwe siyakhishwa kodwa uma kukhokhwe inani elifanele lokukhishwa kwesitifikedi lapho kwenziwa ubhaliso lomshado.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isitifikedi esifingqikwe somshado sikhishwa ngaso leso sikhathi.
Isitifikedi somendo esiphelele (unabridge) sithatha amaviki angu-6 ukuya kwangu-8 ukuthi sikhishwe.

Izindleko

Ukubeletha
Ubhaliso lozalo emva kwesikhathi
Isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) yozalo (ngemuva kwesikhathi)

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ubhaliso lozalo ngemuva kwesikhathi (late registration) kwenziwa lapho ubhaliso lwenziwa ngemuva konyaka ngemuva kokuba ingane izelwe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu le-BI-24/1 lokuzalwa okubhaliswa ngemuva konyaka, kodwa kungakedluli iminyaka engu-15.
Gcwalisa ifomu le-BI-24/15 lokuzalwa ngemuva kweminyaka engu-15.
Fakela ikhophi eqinisekisiwe yedokhumende kamazisi kamama, uma abazai bengashadile noma kanye nekababa yokuvuma ubuzali. Kanti uma ubaba emukela ubuzali, fakele amakhophi aqinisekisiwe abo bobabili abazali, amadokhumende kamazisi. Uma beshadene nomama wengane ngokuzalwa, amakhophi aqinisekisiwe abo bobabili abazali amadokhumende kamazisi kanye nesitifikedi somshado wabo siyadingeka noma
Fakela isitifikedi esivela esibhedlela noma semathenithi lapho ingane eyazalelwa khona. Isitifikedi kumele sisayinwe ngumuntu ophethe indawo (UNsumpa noma uMetroni) kanti kumele sibe negama elisemthethweni lesikhungo kanye nesitembu.
Fakela isiqiniso semininingwane yengane njengoba ikhonjiswe kurejista yesikole noma kwisitifikedi sesikole ingane eyaqala kuso, esisayinwe nguprinsipali (uthishanhloko). Isiqiniso kumele sibe nenombolo kaprinsipali kwi-letterhead enesitembu sesikole nosuku.
Fakela nesitifikedi sengane sokubhabhadiswa, uma sakhishwa eminyakeni emihlanu ingane izelwe.
Uma abazali bengekho, isitatimende esefungelwe kwabesemthethweni i-afidavithi yesihlobo esisondelene kakhulu naye, kodwa esineminyaka emidala enganeni okungenani ngeminyaka engu-10, kanti futhi isihlobo esazi kabanzi ngemininingwane yokuzalwa kwengane, okumele kuhambisane nobunjalo nesimo sengane.
Fakela isitatimende esivela kumuntu onalwazi ngabazali bomenzi wesicelo buqu. Lo muntu kuzomele ahambe nomenzi wesicelo ehhovisi lezaseKhaya lapho umenzi wesicelo kanye nalowo muntu bezophengululwa khona ngemibuzo emagumbini ehlukene (interviewed separately).
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izicelo zingathatha izinyanga ezingu-3 ukuya kwezingu-6.

Qaphela:
Amafomu awatholakali kulayini we-internethi. Sicela uxhumane nehhovisi eliseduze lonyango.

Izinkonzo Kubantu
Ukubhekana nokufa
Ifa lomuntu oshonile
Isicelo somuntu obekwe yinkantolo njengohluleka uk
Isicelo somuntu obekwe yinkantolo njengohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu (insolvent) ukuthi abuyiselwe esimweni esihle (rehabilitation)
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
umuntu obekwe njengohluleka ukukhokha izikweledu ngokwakhe i-agent egunyazwe ngokufanele yomuntu ohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu, uma lowo muntu ohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu engahlali eNingizimu Afrika umfelokazi noma umfelwa womuntu ohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu, uma beshade ngokuhlanganyela impahla (in community of property)
Umuntu angenza isicelo sokubuyiselwa kwisimo esihle (rehabilitation) ngemuva kwesikhathi esingakanani lapho emuncwe impahla yakhe yafakwa ngaphansi kolawulo (sequestrated)?
Noma kunini:
o masishane ngemuva kokuba izindleko zabakweledwayo zikhokhelwe ngokugcwele noma o uma umuntu obekwe njengohluleka ukukhokha izikweledu (insolvent) enze isicelo sokukhokha isikweledu ngokugcwele (offer of composition), nakhona isicelo sakhe sivunyelwe ngabangamakota amathathu abakweledayo, kanti obonwe njengohluleka ukukhona isikweledu ekhokhe izikweledu noma enze isiqinisekiso sokukhokha.
Ngemuva kwezinyanga eziyisithupha:
o uma ezinyangeni eziyisithupha, kungazange kube nombango wesikweledu ngefa lobonwe njengohluleka ukukhokhela isikweledu kanti futhi ngaphansi kwesimo sokuthi lokhu bekungokokuqala umuntu obenwe njengohluleka ukukhokha isikweledu ebekwe ngaphansi kolawulo lokuthathelwa impahla kanti futhi umuntu obenwe njengohluleka isikweledu engenawo amanye amacala ake atholakala enawo.
Ngemuva kwezinyanga eziyishumi nambili :
o ukusukela ngosuku u-Master aqinisekisa ngalo i-akhawunti yolawulo lwempahla yakhe; kanti futhi ngaphansi kwesimo o sokuthi lokhu bekungokokuqala umuntu obenwe njengohluleka ukukhokha isikweledu ebekwe ngaphansi kolawulo lokuthathelwa impahla kanti futhi o umuntu obenwe njengohluleka isikweledu engenawo amanye amacala ake atholakala enawo.
Ngemuva kweminyaka emithathu:
o ukusukela ngosuku u-Master aqinisekisa ikhawunti yolawulo lwempahla o kanti futhi uma obonwe njengohluleka ukukhokha izikweledu eseke wathathelwa amanyathelo olawulo lwempahla ngokuhluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu okungaphezu kokukodwa, kodwa ngaphansi kwesimo sokuthi obonwe njengohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu akakaze atholwe enecela kumacala athize.
Ngemuva kweminyaka emihlanu:
o kwisimo lapho obonwe njengohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu, eseke watholakala enecala kumacala athize.
o ngemuva kokuphela kweminyaka elishumi, obonwe njengohluleka ukukhokhela izikweledu uyobonelelwa ukuthi abuyiselwe kwisimo esihle - ngaphandle kokuba imiyalelo yenkantolo ibeke ngokunye, lapho abantu abathintekayo benze isicelo. Isicelo kumele senziwe kungakapheli iminyaka elishumi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Ungenza isicelo sokubuyiselwa kwisimo esihle ngokwakho, kodwa uzodinga usizo ngezomthetho.
Xhumana nommeli noma nehhovisi lika-Master ukuthola olunye ulwazi olunabile kanye nosizo.

Inzuzo kubantu abakhubazekile
Izinkonzo zokuhlengwa kwabantu abakhubazekile
Izinkonzo zokuhlengwa kwabantu abakhubazekile

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Izinkonzo zokuhlenga (rehabilitation services) zinikezwa abant abakhubazekile ukuze bakwazi ukuzisiza ukufinyelela izinga lokuthi bakwazi ukusebenza ngendlela okungafinyeleleka kuyo. Kukhona imikhakha eminye yezinkonzo zokuhlenga, ezinikezwa kwimikhakha ehlukile yokukhubazeka, kanti lezi zinkonzo yilezi ezilandelayo:
Izinkonzo zokuhlenga emkhakheni weprayimari (wokuqala)
Izinkonzo zokuhlela zeprayimari ngezezinkonzo zokuvimbela (preventative services) ezakha ikhono leklayenti ukuthi ikwazi ukuzenzela. Lokhu kubandakanya i-therapeutic counselling (izeluleko ngokuhlenga nokwelashwa ukuphungula izinhlungu noma ubunzima), izinkonzo zokunakekelwa emakhaya, amaqembu ezosekelo kanye nokuthi abantu bakwazi ukuzenzela, ukuqwashisa ngolwazi kanye nezinhlelo zokumela nokugqugquzelela (advocacy programmes).
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Vakashela endaweni eseduzane nawe yonakekelo lwezempilo noma ikliniki ecubungulayo (screening), kanti khona uzothunyelwa endaweni yohlengo esibhedlela noma kwenye indawo yohlengo lwesiphesheli, lwesekondari noma lwezinga le-tertiary.
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Kungathatha cishe izinyanga ezingu-3 ukubhekana nesicelo sakho. Kwezinye izindawo, abantu bafakelwa kuhla lokulinda izinyanga ezingu-6.
Izindleko
Kwezinye izimo, imali yosizo lukahulumeni kwabakhuzekile efinyelela ku-75% ithathwa njengengxenye yezindleko. Ezinye izindleko zibekwa ngamafasilithi ngokwawo. Amanye amafasilithi akala izindleko ngokuya ngalokho umndeni ongakwazi ukukukhokhela. Zonke lezi zindleko zihlukile kuye ngesifundazwe (ngeprovinsi).
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana

Imvume kubantu abathathe umhlalaphansi
Isicelo semvume yomuntu osethathe umhlalaphansi

Izincazelo
Imvume yomuntu osethathe umhlalaphansi inikezwa umuntu wangaphandle onenhloso yokuzodlela umhlalaphansi eNingizimu Afrika.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma ufisa ukusebenza, kumele uchaze ukuthi akekho umuntu oyisakhamuzi saseNingizimu Afrika noma omhlali okwazi ukwenza umsebenzi waleso sikhundla owenze isicelo saso.
Khipha ikontraki yomsebenzi yenkampani ozoyisebenzela enayo yonke imininingwane efanele ngaleyo nhlangano.
Isitifikedi sogonyo lwesifo se-yellow fever uma uzohamba noma unenhloso yokuhamba udlule endaweni evamise ukuba nesifo se-yellow fever. Isitifikedi ngeke sadingeka lapho uhambe, noma unenhloso yokuhamba udlule ungangeni kuleyo ndawo noma uma isicelo senziwa eNingizimu Afrika.
Khokha idiphozithi. Leyo diphozithi ngeke yabuyiselwa emuva kumkhiphi wayo ngemuva kokuba uhambile noma ngemuva kokuba ukhishelwe imvume. Ukukhokhwa kwediphozithi kungenziwa ngokukhokha ngesheke eligarantwe yibhangi (bank guaranteed cheque) noma nge-credit card ezindaweni lapha okukhona le fasilithi.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izicelo zingathatha isikhathi esifinyelela ezinsukwini ezingu-30.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Phezulu

Izinkonzo ezinhlanganweni
Ukunikeza ulwazi ngokukhokhela intela yesikhashana
Ukunikeza ulwazi ngokukhokhela intela yesikhashana - umkhokho wesithathu
Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Lapho umuntu ethola ingeniso okumele likhokhele intela kodwa elingadonselwa intela ye-SITE noma ye-PAYE (isibonelo, inzalo, irente, noma ingeniso lebhizinisi), kumele akhokhele intela yesikhashana ngaleli ngeniso. Intela yesikhashana (provisional tax) yenziwa njalo ezinyangeni eziyisithupha.
Intela yesikhashana (provisional tax) inenhloso yokusiza abakhokhi bentela ukuhlangabezana nezibopho zabo zentela ngokuqhubekela phambili kunokuthi bakhokhe isamba esikhulu kanye ngonyaka lapho kuhlolwa inani lentela okumele ikhokhekhelwe. Intela yesikhashana izokhokhelwa ukwehlisa inani okumele likhokhelwe kunani lilonke lengeniso (intela evamile) leyo okumele ikhokhelwe ngumuntu siqu kulowo nyaka wezimali ofanele wokukhokhwa kwentela.
Umuntu okumele akhokhele intela yesikhashana (provisional tax), kumele kuthi kungakapheli izinsuku ezingu-30 ngemuva kokuba efanele ayikhokhe, enze isicelo ngokubhala, isicelo sobhaliso (serejistreshini) ehhovisi legatsha labe-South African Revenue Service (SARS). Ukuhluleka ukwenza njalo, kuyoba nomphumela wokukhokhiswa inzalo (i-interest) kanye nama-penalty ngokukhokha sekwedlule isikhathi, kanye nenye futhi intela engezelelwe ehambisana namafomu okukhokhelwa kwentela ngokukhokha kamuva sekwedlule isikhathi.
Umkhokho wesithathu ubuwe waziwe ngoktuhi yi-"additional" noma i-"topping up" wentela yesikhashana. Uma kwenziwa lomkhokho, kumele kukhokhwe kungakafiki usuku olubekiwe (effective date). I-'effective date' isho lapho unyaka wokukhokhwa kwentela uphela ngomhlaka-28 noma umhlaka-29 kuFebruwari, zinyanga eziyisikhombisa ngemuva kwalokhu ekwamukelweni kokuphela konyaka wezimali, ophela ngosuku olungelona umhlaka 28 noma umhlaka 29 kuFebruwari, zinyanga eziyisithupha ngemuva kwalokhu.
Umkhokho wesithathu ngowenziwa ngumuntu okhokha intela yesikhashana ngokuthandela kwakhe njengomkhokhi wentela. Abakhokhi bentela (ngaphandle kwezinkampani) abanemali yentela okumele ikhokhwe engaphezulu kuka-R50 000 noma izinkampani ezin noma izinkampani emali engu-R20 000 noma engaphezulu kwayo bangenza umkhokho wesithathu wokuzithandela (third voluntary payment) ukugwema ukukhokhizwa inzalo efakelwa kulabo abakhokhe intela engaphansi kokumele bayikhokhe (underpayment) lapho kuhlola imali yentela okumele ikhokhwe.
Inhloso yalo mkhokho ukusiza abakhokhi bentela ukuthi bakhokhe umehluko phakathi kwentela yabasebenzi kanye nentela yesikhashana esivele ikhokhiwe yonyaka kanye nenani lilonke lentela okumele ikhokhwe kunyaka wokuhlola imali okumele ikhokhwe (year of assessment).
Isikhathi sesithathu simele inani lilonke lentela okumele ikhokhelwe kunyaka ogcwele, kususwe intela yabasebenzi ekhokhiwe yonyaka ophelele noma yiyiphi enye intela yangaphandle evunyelwe kunyaka ophelele amanani akhokhiwe esikhathini sentela yesikhashana yokuqala neyesibili.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Thola ifomu elisha lokukhokha kwabe-SARS noma uzikhiphele lona kule-website, ebese uligcwalisa ulifakele nomkhokho owukhokhayo.
Ngenxa yokuthi inothisi yokukhokha iyefana kubo bonke nakhona ayifakeli imininingwane yomkhokhi wentela othize, kubaluleke kakhulu ukuthi abakhokhi bentela yesikhashana bafakele imininingwane ye-akhawunti eyiyo ncamashi kuleli fomu. Uma imininingwane eqonde ncamashi ingafakelwanga, umkhokho ngeke wafakelwa uqondaniswe ngqo nomkhokhi wentela.
Imininingwane ye-akhawunti ibandakanya okulandelayo:
Inombolo yereferense yentela yomkhokhi wentela
I-P003 - ekhombisa isikhathi sokukhokha
- ukukhombisa unyaka okhokhelwayo.
Inombolo yereferense efanele, iba kanjena: 1234567123P00032005.
Ungasebenzisa enye yalezi zindlela ukukhokha kwabe-SARS:
Imali yentela yokwesikhashana ingakhokhwa ehhovisi lomnyango wezentela eliseduzane, kusukela ngoMsombuluko kuya kuLwesihlanu phakathi kwehora lesishiyagalombili ekuseni, nehora lesithathu maseligamenxe entambama ngaphandle kwezinsuku ezingamaholide omphakathi.
Uma ngabe abantu bakhokhe ngokuyiposa imali noma bayifake ebhange, kufanele bakhumbule ukuthi bazinikeze isikhathi sokuhamba kweposi noma ukusetshenzwa kwezimali ezifakwe ebhange.
Umangabe imali ithunyelwe nge Computer, kufanele abantu bakhumbule ukuthi ibhange linesikathi elijuba usuku ngalo kanti futhi lidinga isikhathi sokuhlola ukuthi ngabe lomuntu othumela imali unayo yini lemali ebhange, loko kungathatha izinsuku ezimbili kuya kwezinhlanu.
Imininingwane yaseBhange
Inombolo okuzokuthi uma lemali ingena iye kuyona, loku kusho iakhawunti ezokwemukela lemali engenayo.
Yonke imali ekhokhwayo ingavumelani noma ingahambelani naloku okulotshiwe ngaphezulu, ngokwenombolo yomthelo kanye nenombolo yeakhawunti emukelayo, ayingeke imukeleke.
Uma usuku lokugcina lokukhokha lufika kuyiholide noma kuyimpela-sonto, kufanele abantu bakhokhe ngosuku lokugcina lokusebenza olungaphambi kwe holide noma impela-sonto.
Qaphela:
Umkhokho wesithathu (wokuzithandela) kumele ubalelwe phezu kwenani langempela okumele likhokhelwe intela (actual taxable income), ngoba inhloso yalo mkhokho ukusiza ukukhokhela umehluko phakathi kwentela yabasebenzi kanye nentela yesikhashana (plus provisional tax) esikhokhiwe kunyaka kanye nemali ephelele yentela okumele ikhokhelwe (full tax liability) kulowo nyaka wentela.

Izincazelo
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Kwizimo lapho umholo womsebenzi wonyaka uyinani lika-R2 000 noma ngaphansi kwaleli nani, nakhona kungadonswanga intela, kumele kugcwaliswe ifomu le-IT3(a) noma uhla lwabasebenzi olufakelwe nge-alfabethi, olunikeza ulwazi olulandelayo maqondana nomsebenzi ngamunye:
onobumba bokuqala bamagama (initials) nesibongo noma igama ibhizinisi eyaziwa nokusebenza ngalo inombolo yereferense yeNtela yeNgeniso (uma ikhona)
inombolo kamazisi (ye-ID) noma inombolo yepasipoti ikheli inani lomholo (lengeniso)
isikhathi umsebenzi asesisebenzile noma usuku lokugcina ngalo ukukhokhela umholo wonyaka ifomu le-IT3(a) lizonikezwa kulezi zimo ezilandelayo:
Uma wonke umholo womsebenzi noma inani elilingana unyaka, lingaphansi kuka-R2 000 nakhona lingadluli inani okumele lidonselwe intela tax threshold. Uma umsebenzi ebengaqashiwe yilowo mqashi wonke unyaka wentela, umholo otholiwe kulesi sikhathi sentela kumele uhlelwe ngokulandela ingxenye yesilinganiso senani pro-rata ukuze kubonwe ukuthi ngabe kumele likhishwe yini ifomu le-IT3a.
Noma yiyiphi imali ekhokhelwe umuntu ngokunikeza (ngokwenza) inkonzo ethile, noma okumele yenziwe, lapho kungadonswanga khona intela.
MUSA ukunikeza la mafomu maqondana nokulandelayo:
Izimali ezikhokhelwa izinkonzo (amasevisi) athile avamile ekusebenzeni kumaprofeshini, isibonelo, odokotela, abameli bomthetho (amaloya), ama-advocate (amagqwetha), ama-accountant nabacwaningi bezincwadi zezimali (ama-auditor).
ISheduli B: Amafomu e-IT3(b) empahla, izimali ezitshaliwe, amalungelo kanye nentela yengeniso lamalungelo empahla, ezinto ezithile ezenziwa kudala ezifana nokubhala amabhuku nokunye (royalties)
Nikeza imininingwane yala manani akhokhelwe noma atholwe wuwe maqondana:
nenzalo yezimali-mboleko kanye namabhondi empahla ngaphandle kwenzalo yemali-mboleko noma amabhondi okumele akhokhelwe ibhangi nenzalo yezimali ezitshalwe nguwe kwizikhwama ezithile maqondana nenzalo yezitifikedi zemali-mboleko yezinkampani (interest on debentures)
nenzalo yama-akhawunti okulondoloza imali kuma-akhawunti amasheke nawama-savings
Nemali yerente ekhokhwe nguwe njengomqashi noma eqoqwe nguwe njenge-agent
Imali ekhokhelwe ilungelo lokusebenzisa impahla (iprophathi)
imali yezinto ezithile ezenziwa kudala ezifana nokubhala amabhuku nokunye (royalties), noma izimali ezikhokhelwe ukusetshenziswa kwelungelo lobuqambi bezinto ezintsha (patent), idizayini, ilungelo lobunikazi bamagama, uphawu, ubuqambi (trademark), ilungelo elisemthethweni lobuqambi bemibhalo noma ulwazi (copyright), noma lokudlulisa ulwazi oluhambisana nokusetshenziswa kwelungelo lobuqambi bezinto ezithile ezenziwa (patent).
incazelo yeyunithi noma isisetshenziswa kwezezimali inani lamayunithi noma izisetshenziswa ezichithwayo (eziqedwayo)
inzuzo ngemuva kokudonswa kwezimali (net gain) ezidonswayo noma incithakalo (ulahlekelo) maqondana nalezo zisetshenziswa ezichithwayo.
Nikeza ngalolu lwazi:
Zonke izitshalo zepulazi, izingodo (imithi), imfuyo, Insimbi ehlangene namatshe (i-ore), amaminerali, amatshe awumcebo, atholwe wuwe ngokuwathenga, noma ngokushintshanisa ngempahla (barter or exchange) kanti (izinto ozithenga kubathengisi abanamalayisense AKUMELE zifakelwe noma zibandakanywe).
Zonke izitshalo zepulazi, izingodo (imithi), imfuyo, insimbi ehlangene namatshe (i-ore), amaminerali, amatshe awumcebo, athengiswe wuwe njenge-agent yomkhiqizi.
Zonke izitshalo, Insimbi ehlangene namatshe (i-ore), noma amaminerali, ahanjiswe wuwe njenge-agent ehambisa izinto zomtshali kubathengisi abangaphandle kweRiphabhliki.

Ukukhokhela intela yengeniso lezimali ezitshaliwe
Ukukhokhela intela yengeniso lezimali ezitshaliwe, ingeniso lamalungelo empahla, ingeniso eliyisabelo esivela kulungelo lobunikazi bezinto ezithile ezenziwa kudala ezifana nokubhala amabhuku nokunye (royalties)

Izincazelo
Amafomu okukhokhela intela kumele agcwaliswe futhi abuyiselwe uma:
kutholakala inzalo noma ukunqwabelelana (accrued) kwemali yenzalo noma yempahla nesabelo senzalo kumasheya (dividends) ama-Trust empahla uma kutholakala noma ukunqwabelelana isabelo senzalo yamasheya empahla noma imali etshalwe kumazwe angaphandle uma kutholakala noma kunqwabelelane inzalo yamashaye angaphakathi kwezwe kutholakala inani lilonke lerente ekhokhwe ngabaqashi noma eqoqwe ngama-agent asebenzela omunye umuntu maqondana nempahla echaziwe kunemali ekhokhelwe ilungelo lokusebenzisa impahla kunengeniso eliyisabelo esivela kulungelo lobunikazi bezinto ezithile ezenziwa kudala ezifana nokubhala amabhuku nokunye (royalties) noma kunezimali ezikhokhelwa ukusetshenziswa kwelungelo lobuqambi bezinto ezintsha (patent), idizayini, ilungelo lobunikazi bamagama, uphawu, ubuqambi (trademark), ilungelo elisemthethweni lobuqambi bemibhalo noma ulwazi (copyright), noma lokudlulisa ulwazi oluhambisana nokusetshenziswa kwelungelo lobuqambi bezinto ezithile ezenziwa olwazi lwentela ngokunabile.

Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa amafomu e-IT 3: Ukugcwalisa amafomu olwazi lwentela ngokunabile kanye nekhophi yokuqala ye-IT 3(b).
Hambisa la mafomu entela kwihhovisi labe-SARS eliseduzane nawe.

Ukuhlola izimoto
Isicelo sesitifikedi sokufaneleka ukuba semgwaqeni

Izincazelo
Zonke izimoto ezisebenzisa imigwaqo yomphakathi kumele zihlolwe ukuthi ukuze kuqinisekiswe ukuthi zilungele ukuhamba emgwaqweni. Kungumsebenzi womnikazi wemoto ukuthi amikise imoto yakhe iyohlolwa. Ukuhlolwa kwemoto kungenziwa esenta kahulumeni noma esenta ezimele.

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Amanxusa afuna ukuqinisekisa ukuthi izakhamuzi zakuleli eziboshelwe kwamanye amazwe ziphathwa ngendlela eyemukelekile uma zisejele. Ziznto ezihambelana nokunqamulezwa nokuphathwa ngendlela engahlonipheki noma ukujeziswa uzakubikwa kuziphathimandla zakuleyo ndawo. Imithetho yakwamhlabuhlangene yokuphathwa kweziboshwa iyasetshenziswa uma kunje.
Yiluphi usizo olunikezwa ngamanxusa kuzakhamuzi zaseMzansi eziboshelwe kwamanye amazwe? 
Izisebenzi zasehhovisi lamanxusa:
zinikeza isiboshwa ulwazi ngemithetho yalelozwe okungafaka phakathi unqedo lwezemithetho lalelozwe, ukushushiswa uhlu lwabameli (awuqokelwa ummeli) kwenziwa njani uma ubuyiselwe ejele, isimo sebheyili, ukudlulisela icala phambili ukuze isiboshwa sazi ukuthi amalungelo aso ahmba afike kuphi.
ukuhlala ngokuthintana njalo nesiboshwa saseMzansi kulelozwe  babe begcina imithetho yakuleyondawo.
ukuthintana nomndeni nabangane - uma bacelwe yisiboshwa. Akukho kuthintana noma lwazi oluzokushwa ngaphandle kwemvume ngencwadi yesakhamuzi saseMzansi asiboshwe kwelinye izwe.
qinisekisa ukuthi uma udinga usizo lwezempilo utshele iziphathimandla zasejel.
Qaphela:
Igatsha lezangaphandle namanxusa ase South Africa emazweni amanye enza konke okusemandleni awo ukuqinisekisa ukuthi izimali, izincwadi namaphepha okufunda, nemithi ziyafinyelela ngokushesha esiboshweni. Kodwa-ke, imisebenzi ebalulekile yahhovisi lamanxusa yiyona okuqalwa ngayo. Nesimo sezinto kulelozwe singakhinyabeza ukufika kwezinto kusiboshwa.
amanxusa angeke akwazi ukusiza isiboshwa kulezizinto ezilandelayo:
ukugxamalazela ekuqubekeni kwezinkntolo zamazwe ukukutholela ummeli noma ukunikeza izeluleko kwezomthetho ukukucelela ukuba ukhululwe, noma ibheyili ukuya ezindaweni noma amajele ayingozi ukuphenya icala ukukutholela ukuba uhlale kangcono ejele kunabanye abantu ngaphandle uma ngabe imithetho amhlabuhlangene yokugcinwa kweziboshwa ingalandelwa.
ukunikeza abomndeni umyalo wokuthi bathumele imali ukukubhadalela imali yabameli, neyemithi kanye nezinye izindleko ukukutholela indawo yokuhlala, umsebenzi, ivisa noma iphomedi yokuhlala kwelinye izwe ukukhokhela imisebenzi eyenziwe abezokuvakasha, bezindiza, amabhange noma abaqashisa izimoto ukwelekelela ngokomthetho abantu abayizakhamuzi emazweni amabili ezweni lesibili ukukhokhela ukulungisa, nokungqwaba, nokushisa isidumbu somuntu wase South Africa obhubhile
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Abantu abayizakhamuzi zamazwe amabili baboshwe ezweni lesibili lapho bayizakhamuzi nakhona abangeke basizwe ngamanxusa ase South Africa. Uma abantu abayizakhamuzi zamazwe amabili baboshwe kwelinye izwe lapho bengezona izakhamuzi, babe behamba ngeposipoti yezwe lesibili, kumele bathintane nanxusa lalelozwe ababehamba ngepasipoti yakhona.

Izincazelo
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Usizo kubantu abaxekekile, olwesikhashana, lunenhloso yokusiza abantu abakusimo esinzima kakhulu esenza ukuthi bangakwazi ukuhlangabezana nezidingo ezibaluleke kakhulu zemindeni yabo.
Ngubani ongathola usizo lwabantu abaxakeke kakhulu?
Ungathola usizo lokuxakeka kakhulu kuhulumeni uma ukusimo esinzima nesiphuthumayo. Lokhu kungaba ngenxa yokulandelayo:
udinga usizo ngesikhathi ulindele umxhaso (usizo) lwesondlo sengane ngesikhathi uhulumeni esabhekana nesicelo kwenzeke inhlekekele ephuthumayo, enjengokusha kwendlu yakho awukufanelanga ukuthola umxhaso (usizo) lukahulumeni nakhona ukusimo sokuxakeka kakhulu awukwazi ukusebenza ngenxa yokuthi impilo yakho ayikuvumeli - lokhu kusho ukuthi ugula isikhathi esingaphansi kwezinyanga eziyisithupha awukwazi ukuthola isondlo sengane noma izingane zakho komunye umzali wengane owondla umndeni ushonile owondla umndeni usejele okwesikhashana (isikhathi esingaphansi kwezinyanga eziyisithupha)
uphazanyiswe yinhlekelele, kodwa indawo yakini noma umphakathi wakini awumenyezelwanga njengendawo yenhlekelele.
Yini okutholayo?
Usizo kubantu abaxekekile (Social Relief of Distress), lunganikezwa ngamaphasela okudla, noma ivawusha yokuthenga ukudla. Ezinye izifundazwe (amanye amaprovinsi) anikeza lolu sizo ngemali ewukheshi. Usizo kubantu abaxakekile lunikezwa isikhashana esifishane kuphela - kuvamise ukuthi kube zinyanga ezintathu, kokunye izinyanga eziyisithupha .
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Yenza isicelo sosizo kubantu abaxakekile ehhovisi eliseduzane nawe leNtuthuko yaBantu.
Hambisa nalezi zinto lapho wenza isicelo:
Lamaphepha alandelayo azofuneka ukuze ufake isicelo uma incwadi yomazisi noma isitifiketi singekho:
isitetimente esifungelwe ngumuntu ohloniphekile emphakathini owazi umfaki sicelo. Kungaba ilungu lekhansela, umholi wendabuko, unonhlalakahle, noma umfundisi ubufakazi bokuthi isicelo sesitifiketi sokuzalwa noma somazisi sifakiwe negatsha lezasekhaya incwadi yomazisi yesikhashana ekhishwe yigatsha lezasekhaya uma ngabe kunjalo isitifiketi sokubhabhadiswa ikhadi lase kliniki umbiko wesesikoleni.
Kumele unikeze nobufakazi bokuthi:
o wenze isicelo somxhaso (sosizo) lukahulumeni o ube nenhlekelele yesimo esiphuthumayo (isibonelo, ukunikeza umbiko wamaphoyisa wokuthi indlu yakho ishile)
o uzamile ukuthola isondlo o awunalo olunye usekelo olutholayo o ushadile, udivosile (nehlukene) noma uwedwa o awunawo omunye umholo (ingeniso)
Ugula okwesikhashana.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isicelo sakho sizobonelelwa masishane.
Lapho usufakele isicelo, kuzohlolwa ubuqotho baso kanye nokuthi ngabe ngempela udinga usizo.
Ngisho noma ungenawo onke amadokhumende (zonke izincwadi zomthetho ezidingekayo), uzonikezwa iphasela yokudla, ivawusha, noma imali ewukheshi enyangeni yokuqala.
Khumbula ukuthi uhambe nawo wonke amadokhumende kusikhulu somnyango ngaphambi kokuba uthole usizo enyagneni yesibili. Uma ungakwenzi lokho, ngeke wathola usizo lokudla, ivawusha noma ukheshi enyangeni yesibili neyesithathu.
Uma izinto zingaguquki empilweni yakho, ngemuva kokuba uthole usizo lwezinyanga ezintathu, ungenza isicelo sokuthi kungezelwe ezinye izinyanga ezintathu zosizo.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo ayikhokhelwa ngeyamahhala.

Izincazelo
Kumele kufakelwe isicelo lapho umuntu efinyelele iminyaka yomhlalaphansi nakhona efisa ukusebenzisa ama-benefit aqokelelene ngokulandela umgomo womhlalaphansi.
Lesi sicelo singenziwa yilunga elifisa ukudlulisa (transfer) ama-benefit alo ngaphansi kwesinye isikhwama somhlalaphansi ukuya kwesinye isikhwama somhlalaphansi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Kufuneka lolu lwazi ukuze kubonelelwe isicelo somyalelo wentela:
o isibongo somkhokhi wentela o unobumba bamagama (initials) omkhokhi wentela o isitifikedi sokuzalwa somkhokhi wentela o inombolo kamazisi yomkhokhi wentela noma enye intombolo engavamile o uhlobo lomyalelo ocelwayo o isizathu sokucela umyalelo wentela o ikheli lendawo ahlala kuyo kanye nekhodi leposi yomkhokhi wentela o ikheli leposi kanye nekhodi leposi lesikhungu noma lomqashi o umholo wonyaka womkhokhi wentela.
Uma inombolo yereferense yentela ingakhishwanga, nikeza izizathu zokungabhalisi.
Indlela elula yokwenza isicelo somyalelo wentela, wukusenza nge-inthanethi kwi-website ethi: eFiling. Isicelo sakho somyalelo wentela ngale ndlela sivamise ukuvunyelwa kungakapheli amahora angu-48.

Izincazelo
Ilayisensi yokushayela okwesikhashana inikezwa abantu abakwazi ukushayela kodwa:
o balindele ilayisensi yabo eyikhadi o bafake isicelo sokuvuselela ilayisensi yabo o balahlekelwe yilayisensi yabo.
Lelayisensi iyaphela emuva kwezinyanga eziyisithupha kanti itholakala kuwo onke amasenta okuvivinyela ukushayela.

o umazisi wakho noma ipasipoti o izithombe ezingezona zombala zencwadi yomazisi ezimbili.

Imibandela nezimo zokuSebenzisa
Imibandela nezimo zokuSebenzisa
Noma yimuphi umuntu osebenzisa le-website (umsebenzisi) kumele alandele nokuvuma ukulandela imibandela nezimo zokusebenzisa njengoba kubekwe kule nothisi yomthetho Uma umsebenzisi engafisi ukuphoqelelwa yile mibandela nezimo umsebenzisi akumela angene kwi-website, akhombise izinto ezikuyo, asebenzise, noma akhiphe izinto kuyo kanye/noma ukwenza ikhophi noma ukusabalalisa okuqukethwe kule website.
Indlela yokusebenzisa kanye nelungelo lobunikazi (copyright)
Abasebenzisi bangabuka, benze amakhophi, bakhiphele (download) kumakhompyutha abo, baprinte, basabalalise (distribute) okuqukethwe kule website, noma ingxenye yayo ukusebenzisa ulwazi noma ukuchaza lapho abakuthole khona (reference) kuphela, kanti futhi lokhu bangakusebenziseli inzuzo ngezemali.
Konke okuqukethwe lapha, i-data, ama-trademarks (uphawu olubhaliswe emthethweni lwenhlangano noma inkampani)
Lolu lwazi kule website lunenhloso yokunikeza ngolwazi olunabile maqondana nesihloko esithile noma izihloko, kanti futhi alunikeze yonke injula ngalesi sihloko (ngalezi zihloko).
Uma umsebenzisi edinga noma yiluphi ulwazi kwabe-GCIS maqondana nezinkonzo (amasevisi) abo, noma ngokuqukethwe kule-website, kungaxhunyanwa nabo ngokusebenzisa inqubo yokuveza imibono lapha kwi-feedback link
Ukuvikeleka
Ukuphepha
Abe-GCIS banamazinga afanele kwezentengo yetheknoloji kanye nenqubo yokuvikeleka ekusebenzeleni ukuvikela lonke ulwazi olunikezwa ngabasebenzisi ukuthi lungalahleki, lusetshenziswe kabi, lushintshwe noma lubhidlizwe. Kuzothathwa zonke izinyathelo ezifanele ukuvikela ulwazi lomsebenzisi. Abasebenzi abagunyaziwe ababhekene nokunakwa kolwazi oluyimfihlo (sensitive data) olunikezelwe, kumele bagcine imfihlo yalolo lwazi. Umgomo usebenza kubo bonke abasebenzi bakwa-GCIS, noma izinhlangano zikahulumeni ezingamukela ulwazi oluvela kwabe-GCIS. Kuvinjelwe wumthetho kunoma yimuphi umuntu, ibhizinisi, inhlangano, ukuthola, noma ukuzama ukuthola ukungena ngendlela engekho emthethweni kunoma yiliphi ikhasi kule-website, noma ukufakela noma ukuzama ukufakele noma yiyiphi ikhodi engagunyaziwe, elimazayo, noma yolunya kule website. Uma umuntu efakela noma ezama ukufakele ikhodi engagunyaziwe, elimazayo noma yolunya kule website, noma ezama ukungena ngendlela engekho emthethweni kunoma yiliph ikhasi , lowo muntu kule website, lowo muntu uyobekwa icala lokweqa umthetho, kanti uma abe-GCIS noma yimuphi umkhakha kahulumeni ulimazeka noma ngayiphi indlela, uyovula icala lembuyekezo ngokulinyalelwa.
Ukuba yimfihlo (okwangasese)
Isingeniso
Isitatimende semfihlo (sokugcina okwangasese) sisebenza kwabe-GCIS kanye nakunoma yiyiphi inhlangano ebhalise ngokomthetho elawula, eqinisekisa noma esebenzisa ulwazi olungaqokelelwa ngokusebenzisa le-website.
Umsebenzisi angavakashela le website ngaphandle kokunikeza ulwazi ngaye siqu. Kodwa umsebenzisi uvuma ukusebenzisa lolo lwazi (i-data) ngokulandela lesi sitatimende semfihlo (sokugcina okwangasese) Sicela uqaphele ukuthi le website ingaba nokuxhumana namanye ama-website angalawulwa yilesi sitatimende semfihlo (sokugcina okwangasese).
Ukusebenzisa ulwazi lomuntu siqu
Ngisho noma abe-GCIS benganikeza ulwazi lwabasebenzisi kwabanikeza ngezinkonzo (service providers) abasebenza ngowlazi egameni labo, ngeke banikeza abantu besithathu ulwazi, noma eminye imikhakha kahulumeni ukuze ilusebenzisele ezinye izinhloso noma izinhloso ezingaqondene, ngaphandle kokuba lokho kuye kwadalulwa ngesikhathi sokuxhumana.
Ulwazi lomuntu siqu lungadluliselwa kumikhakha yamazwe omhlaba ngabe-GCIS noma kubantu besithathu ngezinhloso ezingenhla. Lokhu ngeke kwabandakanya ukudluliseka kumazwe angenayo imitheshwana yolawulo (rules) lokuvikelwa kolwazi efana naleyo ekhona kuzwe okuhlala kulo umsebenzisi. Ngokunikeza ulwazi kwabe-GCIS ngale website, umsebenzisi uvumela ukudluliselwa kwalolo lwazi.
Ukufinyelela kulwazi lomuntu siqu
Uma kunikezwe ngolwazi lomuntu siqu kule website, abasebenzisi abafisa ukubuyekeza ulazi lwabo ziqu bangenza lokho ngesicelo sokucela ukubuyekeza ngokusebenzisa i-feedback link. Ngemuva kwalokho, abasebenzisi bazokwazi ukufinyelela ama-profile abasebenzisi, ukuqondisa lolo lwazi kanye nokufakela imininingwane yabo yakamuva noma ukukhipha amagama kuhla (unsubscribe) noma kunini. Abasebenzisi abanenkinga yokuthola ama-profile abo, noma abafuna ukucela ikhophi yolwazi lwabo ziqu, kumele baxhumane no-web administrator ngokusebenzisa i-feedback link. Kuzo zonke izimo, abe-GCIS bazobhekana nezicelo zokufinyelela kulwazi, noma ukushintsha ulwazi ngokulandela izidingo zomthetho ezisebenza kulesi sigaba.
Akukho okutholakala lapha ngaphakathi, okubanga noma okunenhloso yokubanga ikontraki noma isivumelwano phakathi kwabe-GCIS kanye nomsebenzisi ovakashela i-website.
Ukuvikela nokugcina imfihlo yangasese yeziNgane
Abe-GCIS bamukela ukubaluleka kokuvikela imfihlo yangasese yezingane ekuxhumaneni ngokwe-online kuwo wonke umhlaba.
Ukunqamula ubudlelwane
Lezi zimo nemibandela zokusetshenziswa ziyisisekelo esiphelele sobudlelwane obuprofeshinali, noma obunye ubudlelwane phakathi kwabe-GCIS kanye nale-website. Noma yikuphi ukuhluleka ngasohlangothini lwabe-GCIS ukusebenzisa noma ukulandela noma yiliphi ilungelo noma okubekiwe (provision) kule mibandela nezimo zokusebenzisa ngeke nanganoma yiyiphi indlela kwaba wukugudlulela eceleni kwalelo lungelo noma lokho okubekiwe Uma umbandela othize noma isimo esithize sokusebenzisa le website singasebenziseke ngkugcwele noma singasebenziseki (valid) ngenxa yanganoma yisiphi isizathu, lowo mbandela (mibandela) noma leso simo (lezo zimo) ziyonqumeka kweminye imibandela nezimo. Imibandela nezimo ezisele ngeke zachaphazeleka ngalokho kunqumeka noma ukungasebenziseki, nakhona kuyohlala kusebenza.
Ukubuyekeza kabusha le Mibandela kanye Nezimo
Abe-GCIS benelungelo lokushintsha (ukuguqula) ukuhlela ngenye indlela (modify), ukungezela noma ukususa izingxenye ezithile noma yonke le mibandlela nezimo zokusebenzisa ngezikhathi nezikhathi. Izinguquko kule mibandela nezimo zokusebenzisa ziyoqala ukusebenza ngaleso sikhathi sezinguquko lapho zifakelwa kule website. Kuyisibopho esisemahlombe omsebenzisi ukuthi ngezikhathi nezikhathi ahlole imibandela nezimo zokusebenzisa le websiste noma izinguquko noma ulwazi olusha olusanda kufakelwa. Ukuqhubeka komsebenzisi ukusebenzisa le website, ngemuva kokufakelwa kwezinguquko noma ulwazi olusha, kuyothathwa ngokuthi lowo msebenzisi uyitholile inothisi kanti futhi wemukela ukuzibophezela ngayo le mibandela nezimo zokusebenzisa, okubandakanya izinguquko nolwazi olusha.

Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Izimbewu okumele kutholakale amasampuli azo, zikhethwa ngokulandelayo:
o ukuthatha nje ngokukhetha kuphi nakuphi (random)
o izimbewu ezisolisayo o izinhlobo zezitshalo ezinezinkinga.
Abahloli bayaziswa ngemiphumela.
Uma imiphumela ibonisa ukuthi izimbewu azihambisani nomthetho, zingahoxiswa ezimakethe ukuthi zingathengiswa noma zingafakelwa amalebuli.
Njengoba lokhu kuhlolwa kwezimbewu kwenziwa ngokulandela umthetho kazwelonke, akukhokhiswa lutho. Kodwa, uma umuntu lapho okuthathwe khona isampuli ecela imiphumela yohlolo, ngendlela yombiko wohlaziyo lohlolo obizwa iSeed Analysis Report, kumele kube nenani lemali evunyele ngabomnyango wezimali okumele likhokhelwe. Kuzokhishwa i-invoice. Imininingwane yeBhangi ingokulandelayo:
Ikhodi yeGatsha: 01-08-45
Inombolo ye-akhawunti: 011244887
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isiteshi esisemthethweni sokuhlola izimbewu (Official Seed Testing Station), sisebenzisa inqubo ebonelela okubalulekile nokumele kubonelelwe kuqala ekuhloleni (priority system). Ngoba izitshalo ezehlukene zinezimo ezehlukene zokuhlolwa, isikhathi sokuhlola singathatha izinsuku ezimbalwa ukuya kumaviki ambalwa. Uma unemibuzo qondana nesikhungu uqobo (bheka imininingwane yokuxhumana).
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo itholakala mahhala.

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Ungabika ngezinye izikhalazo zokuhlolwa kwezimbewu noma izingxabano eziqondene neqophelo lezimbewu, kodwa kumele uqale uxhumane nabe-OSTS (bheka imininingwane yokuxhumana) ukubona ukuthi ngabe ubuchwepheshe obudingekayo ngenhlobo yohlolo kanye/noma ngenhlobo yezimbewu likhona yini. Le nkonzo (le sevisi) itholakala kunoma yimuphi umuntu, inhlangano, isikhungo, inkampani noma umlimi.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma ufuna ukufakela isikhalazo noma ingxabano maqondana neqophelo lezimbewu, thumela incwadi kanye nesampuli yezimbewu kanti futhi ucacise kahle lonke ulwazi maqondana nesikhalazo noma ingxabano. Bandakanya imininingwane yohlolo olwenziwe ngezinye izikhungo zohlolo lwezimbewu.
Uma isikhalazo sibonakala sifanele, isibonelo, iqophelo lezimbewu lingahambisani nokubekwe kumthetho wokuthuthukiswa kwezitshalo, umthetho we-Plant Improvement Act, 1976, akho zindleko. Uma isikhalazo singenalutho olubambekayo nolufanele, kumele kukhokhelwe imali yokuhlola izimbewu ebekwe ngabophiko lwezimali zikaZwelonke (Treasury), kanti kuzokhishwa i-invoice (isigqebhezana sephepha esikhombisa imali okufanele ikhokhelwe).
Kungakhokhwa ngendlela ye-elektroniki (ngokusebenzisa i-inthanethi) noma ngokukhokha kukhawuntala, kodwa kubalulekile ukuthi usho inombolo ye-invoice ukuze bazokwazi ukuqhathanisa imali ekhishiwe kanye ne-invoice eyenziwe. Imininingwane yebhangi ingokulandelayo:
Ikhodi yeGatsha: 01-08-45
Inombolo ye-akhawunti: 011244887
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Abe-OSTS babonelela kuqala ukuhlolwa kwamasampuli ezimbewu lapho kunengxabano noma kungalandelwanga imibandela yomthetho. Ubukhulu bengxabano noma isikhalazo, isibonelo, inani lamasampuli, inhlobo yezitshalo kanye nenhlobo yohlolo okumele kwenziwe, kuchaphazela isikhathi esizothathwa ekuphenyisiseni. Ngeke kwanikezwa ulwazi maqondana namazinga okuhlola. Buza kuleyo ndawo ngokwayo (bheka imininingwane yokuxhumana).
Phezulu
Izindleko
Le nkonzo (le sevisi) ithokalala mahhala uma isikhalazo kungesifanele. Kodwa, uma isikhalazo kungesingafanele (not justified), kuzomele kukhokhelwe imali eyamukelekile yokuhlolwa kwezimbewu, kwisimo nesimo.
Ngu R230 ngehora
Ngu R90 lulunye
Ngu R205 lulunye
Ngu R310 lulunye
Ngu R270 lulunye
Ngu R425 lulunye
Ngu R510 lulunye

Izincazelo
Ukusesha abakhokhela iVAT kutholakala kwi-website nge-inthanethi ngamakhompyutha, kungasetshenziswa abamabhizinisi, amalunga omphakathi, abavakashi kanye neminyango kahulumeni.
Isicelo singasetshenziswa ukwenza isiqiniseko sokuthi ngabe abantu abanikeza ngezimpahla noma izinkonzo (amasevisi) babhalisile yini ne-VAT ukukhuthaza ukuthi abantu babike labo abangakhokheli intela..

Izincazelo
Umzali angenza isicelo sokushintsha isibongo sengane esencane ngokumthetho uma:
ingane yazalwa ngaphandle komendo kanti umama useganeni nomunye umuntu ongeyona ubaba wengane ngokuzala umshado nobaba wengane usuhlakazwe ngedivosi noma ngokushona kanti umama wengane esashada futhi njengomfelokazi, noma odivosile, umama wengane efuna ukubuyela ayosebenzisa isibongo sakubo noma isibongo ayagane kuso kuqala ukuzalwa kwengane eyayizalelwe ngaphandle komshado isibhalise isibongo sikababa wayo ngokwemvelo.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo le-BI-193 kunoma yiliphi ihhovisi lezaseKhaya eNingizimu Afrika. Yenza isicelo kwinxusa (embassy) noma imission uma ungaphandle kwezwe.
Ekhipha imvume ebhaliwe evela kumyeni leyo ngane esithatha isibongo sakhe.
Imvume ebhaliwe evela kubo bobabili abazali iyadingeka kanye nezizathu ezenele ngokubhala, ngesizathu sokushintsha (lapho kusebenza khona).
Imvume kababa wengane ngokuzala iyadingeka, ngaphandle kokuba lokhu kugudlulelwe eceleni yinkantolo.

Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Izindleko
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Uhlelo lwe-Victim Empowerment Programme (VEP) lugqugquzela ukusungulwa kanye nokuxhumanisa izinhlelo nokusebenzisana phakathi kweminyango kahulumeni ehlukene noma kwezinhlelo zemikhakha ehlukene kanye nemigomo yosekelo, ukuvikela kanye nokusiza ngamandla kwabahlukunyezwe wubugebengu nodlame.
I-VEP igxila kakhulu kwabesimame nezingane. Abahlukunyezwe wudlame lwasekhaya abakusimo esiphuthumayo bangahlala endaweni yokukhosela isikhathi esiphakathi kwamaviki amabili nezinyanga eziyisithupha, kuye ngezimo zabo.
Uhlelo lutholakala kwabahlukunyezwe wubugebengu nodlame.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Xhumana nanoma yisiphi isikhungo sabahlukunyeziwe (victim support centre), maqondana nendawo yokukhosela yabesimame abahlukunyeziwe kanye nezingane, ukuthola abanikeza ngenkonzo kahulumeni noma izinhlangano ezingekho ngaphansi kukahulumeni zama-NGO, ezibhekene nonakekelo lwabantu abahlukunyezwe wudlame lwasekhaya.
Umeluleki (counsellor) onikeza ngenkonzo yezempilo, ilunga lenkonzo yamaPhoyisa abe-SAPS, noma isikhulu sephoyisa, usonhlalonhle, uthisha, noma omunye umuntu obhekene nezenhlalakahle yabantu abakhalazayo (abahlukunyeziwe) bangena izicelo zomyalelo wokuvikelwa.
Qinisekisa ukuthi lapho umuntu ongeyena ohlukunyeziwe eletha isicelo somyalelo wokuvikelwa, kukhona incwadi yokuvuma ebhalwe ngumenzi wesikhalazo, ehambisana nesicelo.
Alikho ifomu elibekwe lalokhu kuvuma - incwadi ebhalwe ngumkhalazi yokuvuma yenele. Kodwa-ke, kukhona zimo ezithile lapho khona kungeke kwakhonakala ukuthola incwadi yokuvuma, ezifana nokuthi umkhalazi:
ukhubazekile ngokwengqondo uqulekile noma uma inkantolo ineliseka ngokuthi lowo muntu akakwazi ukwenza ukuvuma ngokwakhe noma ukunikeza imvume.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Isikhathi esidingekayo sokulungiselela umyalelo wesivikelo, sihlukile ngokuya ngekantolo nenkantolo kodwa kuncike ngosuko lapho okuyolalelwa khona udaba lomyalelo wokuvikela enkantolo.
Usonhlalonhle kanye nezinhlangano ezingekho ngaphansi kukahulumeni kumele zenze okulandelayo:
o zisekele abahlukunyezwe wudlame esiteshini samaphoyisa, enkantolo kanye nasezibhedlela, kanye nokusiza umenzi wesicelo ngamandla lapho edinga usizo; kanye o nokunikeza ngolwazi maqondana nenqubo ezolandelwa, amalungelo omuntu okhalazayo, ukuthi uzowuthola kanjani umyalelo wokumvikela kanye nezinye izinto angakhetha kuzo;
Kuwumsebenzi osemahlombe kasonhlalonhle ukwenza okulandelayo:
o ukuthumela ohlukunyeziwe kwezinye izindawo zosizo ezingamsiza, isibonelo, umyalelo wenkantolo, ukuthola ukwelashwa, izinkonzo zokwelulekwa (counselling services), kanye nezinye izindawo zokukhosela;
o ukusiza nge-testimony yobuchwepheshe njengomuntu obhekene nalolu daba owazi zonke izihibe ebudlelwaneni bomndeni;
o ukuba ngumuntu oxhumanisayo enkantolo yomthetho lapho ingane engufakazi khona ngokusebenzisa amasevisi izinkonzo zokuxhumanisa;
o ukusiza ngezinkonzo zokweluleka (counselling service) kumuntu ohlukunyeziwe kanye nakuwo onke amalunga omndeni;
o ukwenza uhlelo lwezokuphepha komuntu noma abantu abahlukunyeziwe (abantu abahlukunyeziwe kumele bakwazi ukusho bona izidingo zabo zezokuphepha kanye nokwenza uhlelo lwabo lokuphepha ngokusizwa ngusonhlalonhle).

Izindleko

Izincazelo
Isosha elidala ngumuntu owalwa impi yomhlaba yokuqala (1914-1918) noma impi yomhlaba yesibili (1939-1945) noma owalwa impi yaseKorea (1950-1953).
Owamukela usizo lwezenhlalakahle, ongakwazi ukuzinakekela kanti udinga ukunakekelwa zikhathi zonke angasifaka isicelo sosizo ngaphezu kwaleso sezenhlalakahle.
Imali yokwelekelela amasosha amadala kusukela ngo April 2008 ingu R960 ngenyanga.
Ukuze ufake isicelo sosizo kumele:
ube yisakhamuzi sase South Africa uphinde uhlale khona ube ngohlala e South Africa ngenkathi ufaka isicelo ube neminyaka engu 60 noma ngaphezulu noma ube ngokhubazekile ube ngowalwa impi yokuqala noma yesibili yomhlaba noma impi yase Korea ube ngowalwa empini eyaqala ngo 6 September 1939
ufezekise izidingo zophenyo lwesimo semali.
Uphenyo lwesimo semaliÂ abantu abayinganisayo ekhaya kuyindlela uhulumeni abona ngayo ukuthi isimo salowomndeni sinjani kwezomnotho. Ukuze uthole imali yokwelekelelwa kumele ukuthi imali oyingeniswa ibe ngaphansi kwenani elithile. Ngo 2008 uphenyo lwemali yokwelekelela amasosha asemadala yomuntu ongashadile yayithi uma impahla yakho ingalinganiselwa emalini engaphezu kwe R451 200, ungeke uthole imali yosizo. Umuzi ohlala kuwona awubalwa uma uhlolwa, kanti nemali oyingenisayo kumele ibe ngaphansi kuka R26 928 ngomnyaka uma ungashadile.
Uma ushadile ungeke uthole imali yokwelekelelwa uma impahla yenu nobabili ingalinganiselwa emalini engaphezu kwe R902 400, Umuzi enihlala kuwo awubalwa uma nihlolwa. Imali yenu nobabili kumele ibe ngaphasi kuka R53 856Â ngomnyaka.
ungabi ngomunye wabagcinwe ezindaweni zikahulumeni ungabi ngomunye wabamukela usizo lwemali kwabazenhlalakahle (ngaphandle kwemali yokunqeda).
ukheshe endaweni ethile ngosuku oluthile ifakwa kwi Akhawundi yomuntu yasebhange (ibhange linelungelo lokukukhokhisa ukwenza lomsebenzi)
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Uma usuluphele noma ugula kakukhulu ukuthi ungahamba uye ehhovisi, cela omunye ukuthi acele abasehhovisi bakuvakashele. Lowomuntu kumele aze nencwadi evela kuwena noma incwadi evela kudokodela eshoyo ukuthi awukwazi ukuza ehhovisi ngoba uyagula. Kungahlelwa ukuthi bakuvakashele.
Thumela ifomu lakho kanye nalamaphepha alandelayo.
o ubufakazi bokuthi uphuma empini kungaba yisitifiketi sempi noma izimendlela zempi.
Lamaphepha alandelayo azofuneka ukuze ufake isicelo uma incwadi yomazisi noma isitifiketi singekho:
o isitetimente esifungelwe ngumuntu ohloniphekile emphakathini owazi umfaki sicelo. Kungaba ilungu lekhansela, umholi wendabuko, unonhlalakahle, noma umfundisi o ubufakazi bokuthi isicelo sesitifiketi sokuzalwa noma somazisi sifakiwe negatsha lezasekhayaÂ 
o incwadi yomazisi yesikhashana ekhishwe yigatsha lezasekhaya uma ngabe kunjalo o isitifiketi sokubhabhadiswa o ikhadi lase klinikiÂ 
Ulwazi ngesimo sakho mayelana nomshado:
o uma ungashadile, iafudaviti esho ukuthi awushadileuma o uma ushadile isitifiketi somshado o uma uhlukene nowakwakho, isitifiketi sokuhlukana o uma owakwakho efile, isitifiketi sokufa.
Ulwazi ngesimo sempahla yakho kanye nemali oyingenisayo:
o uma usebenza, isitifiketi seholo o uma ungasebenzi, incwadi eluhlaza yokukhombisa ukuthi awusebenzi noma isitifiketi sokuphuma emsebenzini esivela kumqashi wakho wakudala o uma inempesheni yangasese, ubufakazi balempesheni o uma uneAkhawundi ebhange, isitatimendi sezinyanga ezintathu ezilandelanayo o uma kukhona lapho utshale imali khona, ulwazi ngenzalo yemail yakho noma isabelo sayo.
Thola irasidi uyigcine njengobufakazi bokuthi ufake isicelo.
Uzokwaziswa ngencwadi ukuthi isicelo sakho siphumelele noma qha.
Uma ungavumelani nesinqumo sabo, faka isikhalo nongqonqgoshe wentuthuko yezenhlalakahle noma ilungu lekhansela (MEC) uchaze ukuthi yini ungavumelani nesinqumo sabo.
Isikhalo kumele sifakwe zingakapheli izinsuku ezingu 90 emva kokuthola impendulo yesicelo sakho.Â 
Phezulu

Indawo nendlela yokuxhumana
Izincazelo
Amanzi ayisisekelo sokuqala amahhala awumthamo we-6kl (6000l) amanzi ngenyanga kumuzi nomuzi. Lo mthamo ungahluka kumasipala nomasipala, kanti kumele uxhumane nomasipala wakini ngqo ukuthola ulwazi ngokuthi yini isevisi (inkonzo) yamanzi ayisisekelo sokuqala amahhala kumasipala wakini. Kumele ukhokhele amanzi owasebenzisayo angaphezulu komthamo ophakelwa mahhala.
Ugesi oyisisekelo sokuqala wamahhala
Abasebenzisi benqubo yokuthenga ugesi abangakawusebenzisi ebizwa ngokuthi yi-pre-paid electricity meters, bazokwazi ukubona lapho ugesi usuphelile, nakhona kuzodingeka bathene omunye futhi. Abasebenzisi bakagesi abawukhokhela ekupheleni kwenyanga noma labo abasebenzisa inqubo ebizwa ngokuthi ama-credit meters, ngeke bakwazi ukubona lapho sebesebenzise onke amayunithi abo. Basoshajwa (khokhiswa) ukusebenzisa ongaphezulu ekupheleni kwenyanga nenyanga.
Abasebenzisi bakagesi abangenawo ugesi wama-grid energy banganikezwa ugesi ongewona ama-grid enegy ngomasipala babo. Kumele uxhumane nomasipala wakini ukuthola ukuthi ngabe yiyiphi inhlobo yogesi eniwusebenzisayo.
Ukuthola ukuxhunyelwa ugesi namanzi endlini yakho noma endaweni yebhizinisi, xhumana nomasipala wakini, lapho onomuzi khona noma onebhizinisi khona.
Kwezinye izndawo, umphakeli kagesi kazwelonke okungu-ESKOM, uphakela ugesi ngokuqonde ngqo.
Izindlu zangasese zamahhala
Uhulumeni akakaqali ukusebenzisa inqubo yohlelo lwezindlu zangasese zamahhala. Umnyango wezaManzi wenza uhlaka lokunikezelwa kwezindlu zangasese zamahhala. Lapho isu seliphethiwe, ohulumeni bezindawo abanikeza ngezindlu zangasese bazokwazi ukusebenzisa lolu hlelo.
Uhulumeni akakaqali ukusebenzisa inqubo yohlelo lokuchithwa kukadoti lwamahhala. Umnyango wezesimo sendalo nezokuvakasha wenza uhlaka lokuchithwa kukadoti mahhala. Lapho isu seliphethiwe, ohulumeni bezindawo abenza imisebenzi yokuchithwa kukadoti bazokwazi ukusebenzisa lolu hlelo.
Ukukhokhela izinkonzo (amasevisi) kamasipala
Kukhona izindlela ezimbili zokukhokhokhela izinkonzo (amasevisi) kamasipala:
Ukukhokhela ama-akhawunti ngaphambi kokusebenzisa ugesi ukuthenga amayuniti angu (kWh/kl) ngemali yakho kusho ukuthi uzosebenzisa kuphela amayunithi owakhokhele noma owathengile. Lapho ususebenzise onke amayunithi, kanti usadinga amanye, kuzomele uthenge amanye.
Ama-akhawunti othola izitatimende ngemuva kokusebenzisa (billed accounts) asho ukuthi ungasebenzisa noma yimuphi umthamo kagesi namanzi. Umasipala wakini uzokuthumela isitatimende njalo ngenyanga esikhombisa umthamo kagesi noma amanzi owasebenzisile kanye nenani lemali okumele uyikhokhe.
Phezulu
Izinyathelo okumele zilandelwe
Hambisa la madokhumende alandelayo:
o ikhophi eqinisekiswe ngabomthetho kaMazisi wakho (se-ID) yakho o ubufakazi bendawo ohlala kuyo noma lapho okukhona ibhizinisi
Gcwalisa ifomu lesicelo.
Khokhela imali yesicelo ebekiwe.
Phezulu
Umthetho osetshenziswayo
Phezulu
Amazinga afanele okwenziwa komsebenzi
Ukubonelelwa kwesicelo sakho kuqala ngosuku ofakela ngalo isicelo sakho sokusekelwa kwabantu abahluphekile (ngezinkonzo zesisekelo zamahhala) Isicelo sakho sizohlolwa ebese kwenziwa isinqumo sokuthi ngabe ukufanele yini ukuthola izinkonzo zesisekelo zamahhla (ukusekelwa kwabantu abahluphekile) noma cha. Ngokuya ngokwenqubo kamasipala wakini, uzobhaliswa ngokufaneleka ukuthola izinkonzo zamahhala ngaphansi kohlelo olubizwa ngokuthi yi-Free Basic Services (FBS), ebese unikezwa usekelo olufanele.
Phezulu
Izindleko
Amanzi: Amalithi okuqala angu-6 000 wamalitha kumuzi ngenyanga, atholakala mahhala, kanti amanye amalitha angaphezulu kwalana ayakhokhelwa ngenani elibekwa ngomasipala.
Phezulu
Amafomu okumele agcwaliswe
Amafomu okusekela abantu abahluphekile (awatholakali kwi-inthanethi). Omasipala abaningi banamafomu ezicelo. Buza kumasipala wakini ngohlelo lokusekela abantu abahluphekile.
